YesBakery presents

The Sourdough Process at YesBakery

Scroll to begin
01 / Ingredients

It starts simple.

Flour, water, salt, and a jar of living starter. Four humble things that, given time, become something extraordinary.

02 / The Mix

Everything comes together.

Flour clouds rise as water pours in. The starter, bubbly and alive, joins the bowl. A salty pinch finishes the gather.

03 / The Dough

Time does the work.

What was a shaggy mass becomes smooth, taut, alive. Folded, rested, folded again. Patience is the only real ingredient.

04 / The Bake

475 degrees and a closed door.

Into the cast iron, into the heat. Steam, crackle, and the slow alchemy of crust forming over crumb.

Fresh bread.

The reward of patience, baked daily at YesBakery.
